{"forms":[{"form":"testaments","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A solemn, authentic instrument in writing, by which a person declares his or her will as to disposal of his or her inheritance (estate and effects) after his or her death, benefiting specified heir(s)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"will"},{"word":"last will and testament"},{"word":"last will"}],"topics":["law"]},{"glosses":["One of the two parts to the scriptures of the Christian religion: the New Testament, considered by Christians to be a continuation of the Hebrew scriptures, and the Hebrew scriptures themselves, which they refer to as the Old Testament."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The ancient aqueducts are a testament to the great engineering skill of the Roman Empire.","type":"example"},{"text":"His remarkable recovery is a testament to the doctor's skill.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A tangible proof or tribute."],"synonyms":[{"word":"testimony"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"The prime minister's speech was a glowing testament to the cabinet's undying commitment to the royal cause.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A credo, expression of conviction."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈtɛs.tə.mənt/","tags":["UK","US"]},{"ipa":"/ˈtes.tə.mənt/","tags":["Australia"]}],"upstream_line":209284,"upstream_offset":1009435735,"upstream_record_sha256":"9ecd7b9ed5d6d5e2cc18b9aa8b9b51a0ae9275bc7ba11f395e11c95e7f8ef441","word":"testament"}
